\frac{1}{|\theta_{1}(\tau,u)|^{2}}=\left|\frac{1}{\eta(\tau)^{3}}\sum_{r\in\mathds{Z}}e^{2\pi iu(r+\frac{1}{2})}S_{r}(\tau)\right|^{2}\ ,\qquad\text{with}\qquad S_{r}(\tau)=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}q^{\frac{n(n+2r+1)}{2}}\ ,

(4.34)

to expand the Theta functions in the denominator,

\frac{1}{|\theta_{1}(\tau,u)|^{2}}=\frac{1}{|\eta(\tau)|^{6}}\sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}S_{r}(\tau)\overline{S_{\bar{r}}(\tau)}\,e^{2\pi is_{1}\tau_{1}(r-\bar{r})}\,e^{-2\pi s_{1}\tau_{2}(r+\bar{r}+1)}\,{\rm exp}\left(2\pi is_{2}(r-\bar{r})\right)\ .

(4.35)

In order to linearize the
s_{2}
dependence, we Poisson resum the last line of (
4.32
),

\hskip-10.0pt\sum_{w,m\in\mathds{Z}}e^{-\frac{\pi\kappa_{sl}}{\tau_{2}}|(s_{1}+w)\tau+(s_{2}+m)-\frac{2}{\kappa_{sl}}z|^{2}}=\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{2}}}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}}\,e^{-\frac{\pi\kappa_{sl}u_{2}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}+\frac{4\pi u_{2}z_{2}}{\tau_{2}}-\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}\,\sum_{w,p\in\mathds{Z}}q^{\frac{1}{4\kappa_{sl}}(p+w\kappa_{sl})^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{4\kappa_{sl}}(p-w\kappa_{sl})^{2}}\\
\times e^{-2\pi i(\frac{p}{\kappa_{sl}}+w)z-2\pi i(\frac{p}{\kappa_{sl}}-w)\bar{z}}\,e^{2\pi is_{1}\tau_{1}p-2\pi s_{1}\tau_{2}w\kappa_{sl}}\,{\rm exp}\left(2\pi is_{2}p\right)\ .

(4.36)

The integral over
s_{2}
is now easy to perform and gives rise to the Kronecker delta

\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,e^{2\pi is_{2}(r+n_{f}^{sl}-\bar{r}-\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}+p)}=\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\ ,

(4.37)

where we made the identifications

(J^{3}_{sl})_{0}-(\bar{J}^{3}_{sl})_{0}=r+n_{f}^{sl}-(\bar{r}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})\ ,

(4.38)

and introduced the gauged current zero modes

\mathcal{J}_{0}=(J^{3}_{sl})_{0}+\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}\,p_{L}}{2}\ ,\qquad\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0}=(\bar{J}^{3}_{sl})_{0}-\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}\,p_{R}}{2}\ ,

(4.39)

with

p_{L}=\frac{p}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}}+w\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}\ ,\qquad p_{R}=\frac{p}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}}-w\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}\ .

(4.40)

We see that the integral over the spatial component of the gauge field zero mode enforces the zero mode of the axial null constraint, much as the integral over the Dehn twist of the torus (the parameter
\tau_{1}
) implements the axial Virasoro constraint in string amplitudes.
The partition function can thus be rewritten as

Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}(\tau,z)=\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{2}}}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}\,|\eta(\tau)|^{6}}\,e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{\kappa_{sl}+2}{\kappa_{sl}}z_{1}^{2}}\sum_{w,p\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\,S_{r}(\tau)\overline{S_{\bar{r}}(\tau)}\\
\times q^{\frac{p_{L}^{2}}{4}}\bar{q}^{\frac{p_{R}^{2}}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}}\pi ip_{L}z-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}}\pi ip_{R}\bar{z}}\,q^{\frac{1}{2}(n_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{2}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\,e^{2\pi i(zn_{f}^{sl}-\bar{z}\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\,\mathcal{I}_{s_{1}}\ ,

(4.41)

where

\mathcal{I}_{s_{1}}=e^{-\pi\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}s_{1}^{2}}\,e^{-2\pi s_{1}\tau_{2}(w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}\ .

(4.42)

Integral over
\bm{s_{1}}
:

The integral over
s_{1}
does not yield a similar delta function imposing the zero mode of the vector null constraint, since the integrand is Gaussian in
s_{1}
. One might worry that this means that unphysical states (
i.e.
not satisfying this constraint) can flow through the torus amplitude; however, we will see that only physical states appear when we decompose the partition sum into characters.

We can perform the integral over
s_{1}
making use of the identity

\sqrt{a}\,e^{-\pi as^{2}}=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,e^{-\frac{\pi}{a}c^{2}-2\pi i\,c\,s}\ ,

(4.43)

which can be checked simply by completing the square. The integral over
s_{1}
then gives

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\,\mathcal{I}_{s_{1}}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\pi\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi c^{2}}{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-2\pi(ic+\tau_{2}(w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}))}}{ic+\tau_{2}(w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\pi\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi c^{2}}{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}}{ic+\tau_{2}(w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}\ .

(4.44)

Let us now consider the first integral in the right-hand-side of (
4.44
) and shift the integration contour from
\text{Im}\,c=0
to
\text{Im}\,c=-\kappa_{sl}\,\tau_{2}
, see Figure
1
.

-i\,\kappa_{sl}\,\tau_{2}

{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\Gamma}

{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Gamma_{dis}}

{\color[rgb]{0,1,1}\Gamma_{con}}

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\text{Im}\,c}

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\text{Re}\,c}

Figure 1
:
The integration contour
\Gamma
(in red) is equivalent to the sum of the contours
\Gamma_{dis}
(in blue) and
\Gamma_{con}
(in cyan),
\Gamma=\Gamma_{dis}+\Gamma_{con}
. This decomposition of integration contours induces the decomposition of the spectrum into discrete and continuous representations of
\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})
.

We can rewrite eq. (
4.44
) as

\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\,\mathcal{I}_{s_{1}}=\mathcal{I}_{dis}+\mathcal{I}_{con}\ ,

(4.45)

with

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{dis}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2\pi\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}\oint_{\Gamma_{dis}}\text{d}c\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi c^{2}}{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-2\pi(ic+\tau_{2}(w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}))}}{ic+\tau_{2}(w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}\ ,

(4.46)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{con}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}_{con}^{+}+\mathcal{I}_{con}^{-}

(4.47)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{con}^{-}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2\pi\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}\,\int_{-\infty-i\tau_{2}\kappa_{sl}}^{\infty-i\tau_{2}\kappa_{sl}}\text{d}c\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi c^{2}}{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-2\pi(ic+\tau_{2}(w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}))}}{ic+\tau_{2}(w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}

(4.48)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{con}^{+}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}\,\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi c^{2}}{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}}{ic+\tau_{2}(w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}\ .

(4.49)

The split performed in eq. (
4.45
) induces an analogous split for the partition function. We rewrite it as

Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}(\tau,z)=Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{dis}(\tau,z)+Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{con}(\tau,z)\ .

(4.50)

While legitimate, this decomposition may seem arbitrary at this stage. However, we will see momentarily that
Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{dis}
and
Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{con}
will respectively give rise to the discrete and continuous spectrum on the worldsheet.

Short string spectrum:

Let us first consider the integral in eq. (
4.46
) and perform the change of variable

c=i\tau_{2}(1-2j_{sl})\ .

(4.51)

We obtain

\mathcal{I}_{dis}=\frac{i}{\pi\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}\oint_{\Gamma_{dis}}\text{d}j_{sl}\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi\tau_{2}}{\kappa_{sl}}(2j_{sl}-1)^{2}}\,e^{-2\pi\tau_{2}(2j_{sl}+w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}}{2j_{sl}+w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}}\ .

(4.52)

This integral can be evaluated by Cauchy’s theorem. As the notation suggests,
j_{sl}
is to be identified with the
\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})
spin. In fact, the region (
4.51
) in terms of
j_{sl}
reads

\frac{1}{2}<j_{sl}<\frac{\kappa_{sl}+1}{2}\ ,

(4.53)

which is the correct Maldacena-Ooguri bound
Maldacena:2000hw
for the short string spectrum. Moreover, poles only appear for
j_{sl}\in\mathds{R}
, again in agreement with the expectation for the short string spectrum. Notice that making the identifications

(j^{3}_{sl})_{0}=r+j_{sl}\ ,\qquad(\bar{j}^{3}_{sl})_{0}=\bar{r}+j_{sl}\ ,\qquad(j^{3}_{f,sl})_{0}=n_{f}^{sl}\ ,\qquad(\bar{j}^{3}_{f,sl})_{0}=\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\ ,

(4.54)

characteristic of the short string spectrum, the denominator of the integrand of (
4.52
) can be written as the sum of the gauged currents (
4.39
)

\mathcal{J}_{0}+\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0}=2j_{sl}+w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\ .

(4.55)

Cauchy’s theorem then gives

\mathcal{I}_{dis}=-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}(q\bar{q})^{-\frac{1}{4\kappa_{sl}}}\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\frac{\kappa_{sl}+1}{2}}\text{d}j_{sl}\,\delta(\mathcal{J}_{0}+\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0})(q\bar{q})^{-\frac{j_{sl}(j_{sl}-1)}{\kappa_{sl}}}\ .

(4.56)

Using equations (
4.5
) and (
4.39
) the
z
-dependent terms in the second line of (
4.41
) can be rewritten as

e^{-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}}\pi ip_{L}z-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}}\pi ip_{R}\bar{z}}\,e^{2\pi i(zn_{f}^{sl}-\bar{z}\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}=e^{2\pi iz(J^{\mathcal{R}}_{sl})_{0}}\,e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}(\bar{J}^{\mathcal{R}}_{sl})_{0}}\ ,

(4.57)

and the short string spectrum partition function reads

Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{dis}(\tau,z)=\frac{e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{\kappa_{sl}+2}{\kappa_{sl}}z_{1}^{2}}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{6}}\,(q\bar{q})^{-\frac{1}{4\kappa_{sl}}}\,\sum_{w,p\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\frac{\kappa_{sl}+1}{2}}\text{d}j_{sl}\,\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\,\delta(\mathcal{J}_{0}+\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0})\\
\times\,q^{\frac{p_{L}^{2}}{4}}\bar{q}^{\frac{p_{R}^{2}}{4}}\,q^{\frac{1}{2}(n_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{2}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})^{2}}e^{2\pi iz(J^{\mathcal{R}}_{sl})_{0}}\,e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}(\bar{J}^{\mathcal{R}}_{sl})_{0}}\,(q\bar{q})^{-\frac{j_{sl}(j_{sl}-1)}{\kappa_{sl}}}\,S_{r}(\tau)\overline{S_{\bar{r}}(\tau)}\ ,

(4.58)

where we reabsorbed various
\kappa_{sl}
and numerical factors into the overall normalization. Recognising the character of
\mathcal{D}^{+}_{j}
representations at level
\kappa_{sl}+2

Pakman:2003kh
,

\chi^{+}_{dis,j}(\tau)\overline{\chi^{+}_{dis,j}(\tau)}=\frac{(q\bar{q})^{-\frac{j(j-1)}{\kappa_{sl}}}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{6}}\sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}S_{r}(\tau)\overline{S_{\bar{r}}(\tau)}\ ,

(4.59)

it becomes clear that the discrete representation contribution to the partition function can be written as a sum of characters over a constrained Hilbert space,

Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{dis}(\tau,z)=e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{\kappa_{sl}+2}{\kappa_{sl}}z_{1}^{2}}\,\text{Tr}_{\mathcal{H}\otimes\bar{\mathcal{H}}}\,q^{L_{0}-\frac{c}{24}}\,\bar{q}^{\bar{L}_{0}-\frac{c}{24}}\,e^{2\pi iz(J^{\mathcal{R}}_{sl})_{0}}\,e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}(\bar{J}^{\mathcal{R}}_{sl})_{0}}\Bigl|_{\mathcal{J}_{0}=\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0}=0}\ ,

(4.60)

where

c=\frac{3(\kappa_{sl}+2)}{\kappa_{sl}}

(4.61)

is the central charge of the
\mathcal{N}=2
cigar theory and the Hilbert space
\mathcal{H}
(similarly for
\bar{\mathcal{H}}
) is obtained by tensoring
\mathcal{D}^{+}_{j_{sl}}
representations with free boson representations. The exponential factor
e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{\kappa_{sl}+2}{\kappa_{sl}}z_{1}^{2}}
in (
4.60
) is the usual prefactor relating path integral and trace partition function, see Appendix
D
.

Long string spectrum:

We are left with the integral
\mathcal{I}_{con}
, see eqs. (
4.47
)-(
4.49
). The basic idea now is to manipulate
\mathcal{I}_{con}^{-}
, see eq. (
4.48
), so that the numerator of the integrand resembles the one in
\mathcal{I}_{con}^{+}
, see eq. (
4.49
). Let us first shift the integration contour of
\mathcal{I}_{con}^{-}
to the real line,

\mathcal{I}_{con}^{-}=-\frac{1}{2\pi\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}\,\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi c^{2}}{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-\pi\tau_{2}\kappa_{sl}}\,e^{-2\pi\tau_{2}(w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}}{ic+\tau_{2}\kappa_{sl}+\tau_{2}(w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}\ .

(4.62)

Consider the associated partition function

Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)},-}_{con}(\tau,z)=\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{2}}}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}\,|\eta(\tau)|^{6}}\,e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{\kappa_{sl}+2}{\kappa_{sl}}z_{1}^{2}}\sum_{w,p\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\,S_{r}(\tau)\overline{S_{\bar{r}}(\tau)}\\
\times q^{\frac{p_{L}^{2}}{4}}\bar{q}^{\frac{p_{R}^{2}}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}}\pi ip_{L}z-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}}\pi ip_{R}\bar{z}}\,q^{\frac{1}{2}(n_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{2}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\,e^{2\pi i(zn_{f}^{sl}-\bar{z}\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}\mathcal{I}_{con}^{-}\ .

(4.63)

Shifting the summation labels by

w\mapsto w-1\ ,\qquad n_{f}^{sl}\mapsto n_{f}^{sl}-1\ ,\qquad\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\mapsto\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}-1\ ,

(4.64)

and exploiting the presence of the Kronecker delta, we obtain

Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)},-}_{con}(\tau,z)=-\frac{e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{\kappa_{sl}+2}{\kappa_{sl}}z_{1}^{2}}}{2\pi\kappa_{sl}\,|\eta(\tau)|^{6}}\sum_{w,p\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\,q^{r}S_{r}(\tau)\bar{q}^{\bar{r}}\,\overline{S_{\bar{r}}(\tau)}\\
\times q^{\frac{p_{L}^{2}}{4}}\bar{q}^{\frac{p_{R}^{2}}{4}}e^{-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}}\pi ip_{L}z-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}}\pi ip_{R}\bar{z}}\,q^{\frac{1}{2}(n_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{2}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\,e^{2\pi i(zn_{f}^{sl}-\bar{z}\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}\\
\times\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi c^{2}}{\kappa_{sl}\tau_{2}}}}{ic+\tau_{2}(w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}-1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})}\ .

(4.65)

Using the identity
Israel:2004ir

q^{r}S_{r}(\tau)=S_{-r}(\tau)\ ,

(4.66)

to change the sign of
r,\bar{r}
and changing integration variable as
c=2\tau_{2}s
we find

Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{con}(\tau,z)=\frac{e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{\kappa_{sl}+2}{\kappa_{sl}}z_{1}^{2}}}{\pi\kappa_{sl}\,|\eta(\tau)|^{6}}\sum_{w,p\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\,S_{r}(\tau)\,\overline{S_{\bar{r}}(\tau)}\\
\times q^{\frac{p_{L}^{2}}{4}}\bar{q}^{\frac{p_{R}^{2}}{4}}\,q^{\frac{1}{2}(n_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{2}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\,e^{2\pi iz(n_{f}^{sl}-\frac{2}{\kappa_{sl}}\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}p_{L}}{2})}e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}+\frac{2}{\kappa_{sl}}\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}p_{R}}{2})}\\
\times\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}s\,(q\bar{q})^{\frac{s^{2}}{\kappa_{sl}}}\left(\tfrac{1}{2is+w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}}-\tfrac{1}{2is+w\kappa_{sl}-r-\bar{r}-1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}}\right)\ .

(4.67)

Making use of the identity
Israel:2004ir

S_{-r-1}(\tau)=1-S_{r}(\tau)\ ,

(4.68)

and neglecting
\kappa_{sl}
and numerical prefactors, we can then rewrite (
4.67
) as

Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{con}(\tau,z)=\frac{e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{\kappa_{sl}+2}{\kappa_{sl}}z_{1}^{2}}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{6}}\sum_{w,p\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\\
q^{\frac{p_{L}^{2}}{4}}\bar{q}^{\frac{p_{R}^{2}}{4}}\,q^{\frac{1}{2}(n_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{2}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\,e^{2\pi iz(n_{f}^{sl}-\frac{2}{\kappa_{sl}}\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}p_{L}}{2})}e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}+\frac{2}{\kappa_{sl}}\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}p_{R}}{2})}\,\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}s\,(q\bar{q})^{\frac{s^{2}}{\kappa_{sl}}}\\
\sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}\Bigl\{\Bigl(\tfrac{1}{2is+w\kappa_{sl}+r+\bar{r}+1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}}-\tfrac{1}{2is+w\kappa_{sl}-r-\bar{r}-1+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}}\Bigr)(S_{r}\overline{S_{\bar{r}}}-(1-S_{r})(1-\overline{S_{\bar{r}}}))\\
+\Bigl(\tfrac{1}{2is+w\kappa_{sl}+r-\bar{r}+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}}-\tfrac{1}{2is+w\kappa_{sl}-r+\bar{r}+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}}\Bigr)(S_{r}(1-\overline{S_{\bar{r}}})-(1-S_{r})\overline{S_{\bar{r}}})\Bigr\}\,\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\ .

(4.69)

The role of
\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})_{n_{5}}
descendants in the path integral formalism is not well understood, see the comments about this in
Israel:2004ir
;
Ashok:2020dnc
. We will then focus on the contribution of primaries, which corresponds to setting
S_{r}(\tau)\to 1
. Proceeding along the lines of
Maldacena:2000kv
;
Hanany:2002ev
and introducing the density of states,

\rho_{cig}(s)=\sum_{r_{+}=0}^{\infty}\Bigl(\frac{1}{2is+r_{+}+1+w\kappa_{sl}+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}}+\frac{1}{2is+r_{+}+1-w\kappa_{sl}-n_{f}^{sl}-\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}}\Bigr)\ ,

(4.70)

the contribution of
\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})_{n_{5}}
primaries reads

Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{con}(\tau,z)\Big|_{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})\text{ prim.}}=\frac{e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{\kappa_{sl}+2}{\kappa_{sl}}z_{1}^{2}}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{6}}\sum_{w,p\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{\frac{p_{L}^{2}}{4}}\bar{q}^{\frac{p_{R}^{2}}{4}}\,q^{\frac{1}{2}(n_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{2}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\\
e^{2\pi iz(n_{f}^{sl}-\frac{2}{\kappa_{sl}}\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}p_{L}}{2})}e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}+\frac{2}{\kappa_{sl}}\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}p_{R}}{2})}\,\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}s\,(q\bar{q})^{\frac{s^{2}}{\kappa_{sl}}}\rho_{cig}(s)\ ,

(4.71)

where we used that for
r_{-}=r-\bar{r}
,

\sum_{r_{-}\in\mathds{Z}}\,\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}=1\ .

(4.72)

The density of states (
4.70
) is strictly speaking diverging and should be regularized. The correct regularization and the associated physics has been explained in detail in
Maldacena:2000kv
, see also
Hanany:2002ev
;
Israel:2004ir
;
Eguchi:2004yi
;
Eguchi:2010cb
;
Troost:2010ud
.
Notice that

\displaystyle e^{2\pi iz(n_{f}^{sl}-\frac{2}{\kappa_{sl}}\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}p_{L}}{2})}

\displaystyle=e^{2\pi iz(J^{\mathcal{R}}_{sl})_{0}}\Big|_{\mathcal{J}_{0}=0}

(4.73)

\displaystyle e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}+\frac{2}{\kappa_{sl}}\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{sl}}p_{R}}{2})}

\displaystyle=e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}(\bar{J}^{\mathcal{R}}_{sl})_{0}}\Big|_{\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0}=0}\ ,

(4.74)

and hence the partition function can be written in a form making manifest the gauge constraints
\mathcal{J}_{0}=\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0}=0
,

\displaystyle Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{con}(\tau,z)\Big|_{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})\text{ prim.}}=

\displaystyle\,\frac{e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{\kappa_{sl}+2}{\kappa_{sl}}z_{1}^{2}}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{6}}\sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\,q^{\frac{1}{2}(n_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\,e^{2\pi iz\frac{\kappa_{sl}+2}{\kappa_{sl}}n_{f}^{sl}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{2}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})^{2}}e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}\frac{\kappa_{sl}+2}{\kappa_{sl}}\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}}

\displaystyle\sum_{w,p\in\mathds{Z}}q^{\frac{p_{L}^{2}}{4}}\bar{q}^{\frac{p_{R}^{2}}{4}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}((j^{3}_{sl})_{0}+(\bar{j}^{3}_{sl})_{0})\sum_{(j^{3}_{sl})_{0}-(\bar{j}^{3}_{sl})_{0}\in\mathds{Z}}

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}s\,\rho_{cig}(s)\,\chi_{c}(s,(j^{3}_{sl})_{0})\,\overline{\chi_{c}(s,(\bar{j}^{3}_{sl})_{0})}\,\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\,\delta(\mathcal{J}_{0}+\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0})\ ,

(4.75)

where we introduced the character counting primaries of continuous
\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})_{n_{5}}
representations

\chi_{\text{c}}(s,m)=q^{\frac{s^{2}}{\kappa_{sl}}}\,\xi^{\frac{2m}{\kappa_{sl}}}\ .

(4.76)

The situation for current algebra descendants is less clear, in particular it has not been shown that their contributions can be written manifestly in terms of a sum over states that satisfy the zero mode of the vector null constraint.

5
\bm{\big(\frac{SL(2,\mathds{R})}{U(1)}\!\times\!\frac{{SU(2)}}{U(1)}\big)/{\mathbb{Z}}_{{n_{5}}}}
coset orbifold

We now come to a more intricate example of null gauging, namely the supersymmetric coset

\frac{SL(2,\mathds{R})_{n_{5}}\times SU(2)_{n_{5}}}{U(1)_{L}\times U(1)_{R}}\ .

(5.1)

Now
H
embeds in the numerator group, but is not a factor in it to be trivially removed by the additional gauge symmetry (
2.32
). We are going to compute the associated partition function

\text{Tr}_{R}\Big[(-1)^{F}q^{L_{0}-\frac{c}{24}}\,\bar{q}^{\bar{L}_{0}-\frac{c}{24}}\,e^{2\pi izJ^{\mathcal{R}}_{0}}\,e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}\bar{J}^{\mathcal{R}}_{0}}\Big]\ ,

(5.2)

where

J^{\mathcal{R}}=J^{\mathcal{R}}_{sl}+J^{\mathcal{R}}_{su}\ ,\qquad\bar{J}^{\mathcal{R}}=\bar{J}^{\mathcal{R}}_{sl}+\bar{J}^{\mathcal{R}}_{su}\ ,

(5.3)

denote the holomorphic and anti-holomorphic
\mathcal{R}
-currents. Also in this case we consider the
R
sector with insertions of
(-1)^{F}=(-1)^{F_{L}+F_{R}}
. As discussed already in Section
2.5
, see eq. (
2.73
), the coset CFT (
5.1
) is believed to be equivalent to the coset orbifold

\left(\frac{SL(2,\mathds{R})_{n_{5}}}{U(1)}\!\times\!\frac{{SU(2)}_{n_{5}}}{U(1)}\right)\Bigr/{\mathbb{Z}}_{{n_{5}}}\ ,

(5.4)

playing a central role in string models of
n_{5}
parallel NS5-branes and little string theory
Giveon:1999px
;
Giveon:1999tq
. The partition function of the coset orbifold (
5.4
) was computed in
Israel:2004ir
;
Eguchi:2004ik
;
Eguchi:2010cb
;
Giveon:2015raa
.
In Section
5.2
we show that the partition function of the null gauge coset CFT (
5.1
) exactly agrees with the known partition function
Israel:2004ir
of the coset orbifold (
5.4
).

5.1
Null gauge coset

Let us now derive the partition function for the null gauge coset (
5.1
), with embeddings

\varepsilon_{L}^{sl}=\varepsilon_{R}^{sl}=-\frac{\sigma_{3}}{2}\ ,\qquad\varepsilon_{L}^{su}=-\varepsilon_{R}^{su}=-\frac{\sigma_{3}}{2}\ .

(5.5)

Following the general discussion of Section
2.4
, the partition function can be written as

Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})\times\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)_{L}\times\mathfrak{u}(1)_{R}}}(\tau,z)=\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,Z_{gh}\,Z^{B}_{sl}\,Z^{B}_{su}\,Z^{F}_{sl}\,Z^{F}_{su}\ .

(5.6)

Since the embeddings (
5.5
) respectively equal the embeddings chosen in Sections
3
and
4
, compare with eqs. (
3.8
) and (
4.4
), it is easy to evaluate the various contributions, see eqs. (
3.15
), (
3.34
), (
4.15
), (
4.16
), (
4.23
) with
\kappa_{sl}=\kappa_{su}=n_{5}
,

\displaystyle Z_{sl}^{B}

\displaystyle=Z^{GK}_{sl}(\tau,u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)\,{\rm exp}\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{n_5+2}{\pi}$}}\mathop{\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\int$}}}\nolimits\kern-0.99998pt\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z])|^{2}\right)\ ,

(5.7)

\displaystyle Z^{B}_{su}

\displaystyle=e^{-\frac{\pi\,(n_{5}-2)}{\tau_{2}}(u_{2}-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{\rm exp}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{n_5-2}{\pi}$}}\mathop{\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\int$}}}\nolimits\kern-0.99998pt\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z])|^{2}\right)\mathcal{Z}_{B}^{\mathfrak{su}(2)}(\tau,u-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)\ ,

(5.8)

\displaystyle Z^{F}_{sl}

\displaystyle=e^{-\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}(u_{2}+\frac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}\frac{|\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z)|^{2}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{2}}\,{\rm exp}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{\pi}$}}\mathop{\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\int$}}}\nolimits\kern-0.99998pt\text{d}^{2}v\,\left|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z])\right|^{2}\right)\ ,

(5.9)

\displaystyle Z^{F}_{su}

\displaystyle=e^{-\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}(u_{2}+\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}\,\frac{\big|\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z)\big|^{2}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{2}}\,{\rm exp}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{\pi}$}}\mathop{\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\int$}}}\nolimits\kern-0.99998pt\text{d}^{2}v\,\big|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u+\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z])\big|^{2}\right)\ .

(5.10)

Gathering all the terms in eqs. (
5.7
)-(
5.10
), together with the ghost contribution, after some algebra we obtain the null gauge coset partition function
14
14

14

Again, we will not be concerned with the overall normalization of the partition function.

\displaystyle Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})\times\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)_{L}\times\mathfrak{u}(1)_{R}}}(\tau,z)=\tau_{2}\,e^{\frac{4\pi}{\tau_{2}}z_{1}^{2}}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,e^{-\frac{\pi(n_{5}+2)}{\tau_{2}}(u_{2}+\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 70.0pt\times\,Z_{sl}^{GK}(\tau,u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)\,\mathcal{Z}_{B}^{\mathfrak{su}(2)}(\tau,u-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z)\,\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z)|^{2}\ ,

(5.11)

where the partition functions
Z^{GK}_{sl}
and
\mathcal{Z}_{B}^{\mathfrak{su}(2)}
have been introduced in eqs. (
4.15
) and (
B.2
) respectively.

5.2
The coset orbifold

In this section we are going to show that the partition function (
5.11
) for the coset CFT (
5.1
) equals the partition function of the coset orbifold CFT (
5.4
). The latter reads
Israel:2004ir

Z^{(\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}\times\frac{\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)})/\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}(\tau,z)=\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2 \, n_5}$}}\sum_{\alpha,\beta\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}Z_{\alpha,\beta}^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{u(1)}}(\tau,z)\,Z_{\alpha,\beta}^{\frac{\mathfrak{su}(2)}{u(1)}}(\tau,z)\ ,

(5.12)

where

Z_{\alpha,\beta}^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{u(1)}}(\tau,z)=e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}(\frac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}|z|^{2}-\frac{n_{5}+4}{n_{5}}z_{2}^{2})}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,\left|\frac{\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z)}{\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)}\right|^{2}e^{-4\pi\frac{u_{2}z_{2}}{\tau_{2}}}\\
\times\,\sum_{m_{1},m_{2}\in\mathds{Z}}{\rm exp}\left(-\frac{\pi n_{5}}{\tau_{2}}\left|u+m_{1}\tau+m_{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\alpha\tau+ \beta}{n_5}$}}\right|^{2}\right)

(5.13)

and
Gepner:1986hr

Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{\alpha,\beta}(\tau,z)=\frac{e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z_{1}^{2}}}{2}\sum_{j_{su}=0}^{n_{5}-2}\sum_{\ell=-n_{5}+1}^{n_{5}}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{n_{5}}(\ell-\alpha)\beta}\,I^{j_{su}}_{-\ell+2\alpha}(\tau,z)\overline{I^{j_{su}}_{\ell}(\tau,z)}\ ,

(5.14)

see Appendix
B
for the definition of the characters
I^{j_{su}}_{\ell}
. The exponential factor
e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z_{1}^{2}}
in eq. (
5.13
) is due to the relation between the trace partition function and the path integral partition function, see eq. (
D.5
). It is a simple exercise to show that

\displaystyle\sum_{m_{1},m_{2}\in\mathds{Z}}e^{-\frac{\pi n_{5}}{\tau_{2}}|u+m_{1}\tau+m_{2}|^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{2}}{n_{5}}}\,e^{-\frac{\pi n_{5}}{\tau_{2}}u_{2}^{2}}\sum_{w,p\in\mathds{Z}}q^{n_{5}(\frac{p}{2n_{5}}-\frac{w}{2})^{2}}\bar{q}^{n_{5}(\frac{p}{2n_{5}}+\frac{w}{2})^{2}}\,y^{\frac{1}{2}(n_{5}w-p)}\,\bar{y}^{\frac{1}{2}(n_{5}w+p)}

(5.15)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{2}}{n_{5}}}\,e^{-\frac{\pi n_{5}}{\tau_{2}}u_{2}^{2}}\,\sum_{\ell\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}\Theta^{(n_{5})}_{\ell}(\tau,-u)\overline{\Theta^{(n_{5})}_{\ell}(\tau,u)}\ ,

(5.16)

where in the first equality we used the Poisson resummation identity
DiFrancesco:1997nk
, while in the second equality we changed the summation variables according to

p=n_{5}(m_{L}+m_{R})+\ell\ ,\qquad w=m_{R}-m_{L}\ ,\qquad\text{with}\qquad\ell\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}\ ,\quad m_{L},m_{R}\in\mathds{Z}

(5.17)

and used the definition (
A.1
). In (
5.17
), by
\ell\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}
we mean that
\ell
can run over any set of
n_{5}
consecutive integers. Using eq. (
5.16
) we can then rewrite eq. (
5.13
) as

Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{\alpha,\beta}(\tau,z)=\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{2}}{n_{5}}}\,e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}(\frac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}|z|^{2}-\frac{n_{5}+4}{n_{5}}z_{2}^{2})}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,\left|\frac{\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z)}{\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)}\right|^{2}\\
\times\,e^{-4\pi\frac{u_{2}z_{2}}{\tau_{2}}}e^{-\frac{\pi n_{5}}{\tau_{2}}(u_{2}+\frac{\alpha\tau_{2}}{n_{5}})^{2}}\sum_{\ell\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}\Theta_{\ell}^{(n_{5})}\left(\tau,-u-\tfrac{\alpha\tau+\beta}{n_{5}}\right)\overline{\Theta_{\ell}^{(n_{5})}\left(\tau,u+\tfrac{\alpha\tau+\beta}{n_{5}}\right)}\ .

(5.18)

Recognising the
SL(2,\mathds{R})
partition function (
4.15
), we find
15
15

15

We introduced the normalization constant
\mathcal{N}
to parametrize our ignorance in the normalization of the SL
(2,\mathds{R})
partition function (
4.15
). Since we have no control on the overall normalization, in the following we will reabsorb various factor of
n_{5}
into
\mathcal{N}
.

\displaystyle Z^{\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{\alpha,\beta}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{N}\,\tau_{2}\,e^{\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}\frac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}|z|^{2}}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,Z^{GK}_{sl}(\tau,u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z)|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\,e^{-\frac{\pi}{\tau_{2}}\left(2\frac{(n_{5}+2)^{2}}{n_{5}^{2}}z_{2}^{2}+(n_{5}+2)u_{2}^{2}+4\frac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}u_{2}z_{2}+2\alpha u_{2}\tau_{2}+\frac{\alpha^{2}\tau_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}}\right)}\,\times

\displaystyle\qquad\times\,\sum_{\ell\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}\Theta_{\ell}^{(n_{5})}\left(\tau,-u-\tfrac{\alpha\tau+\beta}{n_{5}}\right)\overline{\Theta_{\ell}^{(n_{5})}\left(\tau,u+\tfrac{\alpha\tau+\beta}{n_{5}}\right)}\ ,

(5.19)

The coset orbifold partition function (
5.12
) can thus be written as

\displaystyle Z^{(\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}\times\frac{\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)})/\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{N}\,\tau_{2}\,e^{\frac{4\pi}{\tau_{2}}z_{1}^{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}\sum_{j_{su}=0}^{n_{5}-2}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=-n_{5}+1}^{n_{5}}I^{j_{su}}_{-\ell^{\prime}+2\alpha}(\tau,z)\overline{I^{j_{su}}_{\ell^{\prime}}(\tau,z)}

\displaystyle\quad\times\,\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,Z^{GK}_{sl}(\tau,u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z)|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\times\,e^{-\frac{\pi}{\tau_{2}}(n_{5}+2)(u_{2}+\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}-2\pi\alpha u_{2}-\frac{\pi\alpha^{2}\tau_{2}}{n_{5}}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\,\sum_{\ell\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}\sum_{\beta\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{n_{5}}(\ell^{\prime}-\alpha)\beta}\Theta_{\ell}^{(n_{5})}(\tau,-u-\tfrac{\alpha\tau+\beta}{n_{5}})\overline{\Theta_{\ell}^{(n_{5})}(\tau,u+\tfrac{\alpha\tau+\beta}{n_{5}})}\ .

(5.20)

Consider the sum in the last line of (
5.20
). Using the definition (
A.1
), the representation of the Kronecker delta

\sum_{\beta\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}e^{\frac{2\pi i\beta}{n_{5}}(\ell^{\prime}-\alpha-\ell)}=n_{5}\,\delta_{\alpha,\ell^{\prime}-\ell}\ ,

(5.21)

and the identities (
A.5
), (
A.7
), the last line of (
5.20
) can be rewritten as

\sum_{\beta\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}e^{\frac{2\pi i}{n_{5}}(\ell^{\prime}-\alpha)\beta}\Theta_{\ell}^{(n_{5})}(\tau,-u-\tfrac{\alpha\tau+\beta}{n_{5}})\overline{\Theta_{\ell}^{(n_{5})}(\tau,u+\tfrac{\alpha\tau+\beta}{n_{5}})}\\
=n_{5}\,\delta_{\alpha,\ell^{\prime}-\ell}\,e^{\frac{\pi\alpha^{2}\tau_{2}}{n_{5}}}\,e^{2\pi\alpha u_{2}}\,\Theta^{(n_{5})}_{-\ell+\alpha}(\tau,u)\,\overline{\Theta_{\ell+\alpha}^{(n_{5})}(\tau,u)}\ .

(5.22)

Substituting in eq. (
5.20
) we find

Z^{(\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}\times\frac{\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)})/\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}(\tau,z)=\mathcal{N}\,\tau_{2}\,e^{\frac{4\pi}{\tau_{2}}z_{1}^{2}}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,Z^{GK}_{sl}(\tau,u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z)|^{2}\\
\times\,e^{-\frac{\pi(n_{5}+2)}{\tau_{2}}(u_{2}+\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}\,\sum_{j_{su}=0}^{n_{5}-2}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=-n_{5}+1}^{n_{5}}\overline{\Theta_{\ell^{\prime}}^{(n_{5})}(\tau,u)\,I^{j_{su}}_{\ell^{\prime}}(\tau,z)}\sum_{\ell\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}\Theta^{(n_{5})}_{-2\ell+\ell^{\prime}}(\tau,u)\,I^{j_{su}}_{-2\ell+\ell^{\prime}}(\tau,z)\ .

(5.23)

We claim that the sum over
\ell
can be rewritten as

\sum_{\ell\in\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}\Theta^{(n_{5})}_{-2\ell+\ell^{\prime}}(\tau,u)\,I^{j_{su}}_{-2\ell+\ell^{\prime}}(\tau,z)=\sum_{\ell=-n_{5}+1}^{n_{5}}\Theta^{(n_{5})}_{\ell}(\tau,u)\,I^{j_{su}}_{\ell}(\tau,z)\ .

(5.24)

The proof slightly depends on the parity of
n_{5}
and
j_{su}
. Let us first consider
n_{5}\in 2\mathds{Z}
and
j_{su}\in 2\mathds{Z}+1
. From eq. (
B.20
) it follows that we can restrict to
\ell^{\prime}\in 2\mathds{Z}
. Let us choose
\ell
to run over

\frac{\ell^{\prime}-n_{5}}{2}\leq\ell\leq\frac{n_{5}-2+\ell^{\prime}}{2}\ .

(5.25)

Notice that
\frac{\ell^{\prime}-n_{5}}{2},\frac{n_{5}-2+\ell^{\prime}}{2}\in\mathds{Z}
and that there are exactly
n_{5}
integers in the interval (
5.25
). Introducing a new sum label
\ell^{\prime\prime}=-2\ell+\ell^{\prime}
and using again eq. (
B.20
), eq. (
5.24
) follows. The argument for other choices of the parity of
n_{5}
and
j
is completely analogous. Hence, making use of eqs. (
B.2
) and (
B.17
), we finally obtain

Z^{(\frac{\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}\times\frac{\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)})/\mathds{Z}_{n_{5}}}(\tau,z)=\mathcal{N}\,\tau_{2}\,e^{\frac{4\pi}{\tau_{2}}z_{1}^{2}}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,e^{-\frac{\pi(n_{5}+2)}{\tau_{2}}(u_{2}+\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}\,\times\\
\times\,Z^{GK}_{sl}(\tau,u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)\,\mathcal{Z}_{B}^{\mathfrak{su}(2)}(\tau,u-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z)\,\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z)|^{2}\ ,

(5.26)

which, up to the overall normalization, exactly reproduces the null gauged coset partition function (
5.11
).

6
Supertube partition functions

Finally, we come to a discussion of the generalized “round supertube” backgrounds of
Martinec:2017ztd
.
These are null gauged models

\frac{G}{H}=\frac{SL(2,\mathds{R})_{n_{5}}\times SU(2)_{n_{5}}\times\mathds{R}_{t}\times U(1)_{y}}{U(1)_{L}\times U(1)_{R}}\ ,

(6.1)

with the embeddings

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{L}^{sl}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\sigma_{3}}{2}\ ,

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{L}^{su}

\displaystyle=-\ell_{2}\frac{\sigma_{3}}{2}\ ,

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{L}^{t}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\ell_{3}}{2}\ ,

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{L}^{y}

\displaystyle=\frac{\ell_{4}}{2}\ ,

(6.2)

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{R}^{sl}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma_{3}}{2}\ ,

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{R}^{su}

\displaystyle=-r_{2}\frac{\sigma_{3}}{2}\ ,

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{R}^{t}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\ell_{3}}{2}\ ,

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{R}^{y}

\displaystyle=-\frac{r_{4}}{2}\ ,

satisfying the null conditions

n_{5}(-1+\ell_{2}^{2})-\ell_{3}^{2}+\ell_{4}^{2}=0\ ,\qquad n_{5}(-1+r_{2}^{2})-\ell_{3}^{2}+r_{4}^{2}=0\ .

(6.3)

Eq. (
6.3
) guarantees that the gauging is null,

\displaystyle\sum_{i}\kappa_{i}\text{Tr}[(\varepsilon_{L}^{i})^{2}]

\displaystyle=-n_{5}\,\text{Tr}\left[\left(\frac{\sigma_{3}}{2}\right)^{2}\right]+n_{5}\ell_{2}^{2}\,\text{Tr}\left[\left(\frac{\sigma_{3}}{2}\right)^{2}\right]-2\frac{\ell_{3}^{2}}{4}+2\frac{\ell_{4}^{2}}{4}=0\ ,

(6.4)

\displaystyle\sum_{i}\kappa_{i}\text{Tr}[(\varepsilon_{R}^{i})^{2}]

\displaystyle=-n_{5}\,\text{Tr}\left[\left(\frac{\sigma_{3}}{2}\right)^{2}\right]+n_{5}r_{2}^{2}\,\text{Tr}\left[\left(\frac{\sigma_{3}}{2}\right)^{2}\right]-2\frac{\ell_{3}^{2}}{4}+2\frac{r_{4}^{2}}{4}=0\ .

Following the logic of Section
2.4
, the partition function of the asymmetric coset (
6.1
) can be written as

Z^{ST}(\tau,z)=Z_{tr}^{F}(\tau,z)\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,Z_{gh}(\tau)\,Z^{F}_{sl}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z)\,Z^{F}_{su}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z)\\
Z^{B}_{sl}(\tau,u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)\,Z^{B}_{su}(\tau,u-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)\,Z^{B}_{t}(\tau,u)\,Z^{B}_{y}(\tau,u)\ ,

(6.5)

where, in order to include the dependence on the
\mathcal{R}
-charge chemical potential
z
, the chemical potential
u
is shifted as in eqs. (
3.6
) and (
4.6
). In eq. (
6.5
),
Z_{tr}^{F}(\tau,z)
is the contribution of the two uncharged fermions in the “trasverse space”, see the discussion in Section
2.4
and Appendix
E
.
After briefly reviewing in Section
6.1
some properties of supertube cosets, in Section
6.2
we compute the partition function of the supertube coset (
6.1
) in its integral form while in Section
6.3
we decompose it into characters and explain how the gauge constraints emerge from the path integral perspective.

6.1
Supertube cosets

A convenient parametrization of the embedding parameters
\ell_{i}
and
r_{i}
is
Bufalini:2021ndn

\displaystyle\begin{split}\ell_{2}&={\mathsf{m}}+{\mathsf{n}}\in 2\mathds{Z}+1~,~~~r_{2}=-{\mathsf{m}}+{\mathsf{n}}\in 2\mathds{Z}+1~,~~~\\[5.69046pt]
\ell_{3}&=r_{3}=-\sqrt{{\mathsf{k}}^{2}R_{y}^{2}+\frac{{\mathsf{p}}^{2}}{R_{y}^{2}}+n_{5}({\mathsf{m}}^{2}+{\mathsf{n}}^{2}-1)}~,\\[5.69046pt]
\ell_{4}&=-{{\mathsf{k}}}R_{y}+\frac{{\mathsf{p}}}{R_{y}}~,~~~r_{4}={{\mathsf{k}}}R_{y}+\frac{{\mathsf{p}}}{R_{y}}~,~~~~~~~~{\mathsf{p}}=n_{5}\frac{{\mathsf{m}}{\mathsf{n}}}{{\mathsf{k}}}~,\end{split}

(6.6)

where
{\mathsf{m}},{\mathsf{n}},{\mathsf{k}},{\mathsf{p}}\in\mathds{Z}
. For
{\mathsf{m}}=1,{\mathsf{n}}=0
,
the model describes 1/2-BPS states of NS5-branes and fundamental strings in which the NS5-brane source lies at the origin in
SL(2,\mathds{R})
and along a great circle in
{SU(2)}

Lunin:2001fv
. For
{\mathsf{m}}-{\mathsf{n}}=1,{\mathsf{n}}\neq 0
, the background is 1/4-BPS and describes an NS5-F1 bound state carrying a macroscopic angular momentum along
{SU(2)}_{L}

Giusto:2004id
;
Giusto:2012yz
. Finally, general values of
{\mathsf{m}},{\mathsf{n}}
describe non-supersymmetric NS5-F1 states having both left and right
{SU(2)}
angular momentum
Jejjala:2005yu
.

One sees all these features by choosing a gauge that fixes two of the coordinates of
G
, and then integrating out the gauge field to arrive at an effective sigma model action from which one reads off the metric and
B
-field. The schematic form of the gauge field dependence in the action (
2.19
) is

\int\big(\mathcal{A}\bar{\mathcal{J}}+\bar{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{J}+\Sigma\mathcal{A}\bar{\mathcal{A}}\big)~,

(6.7)

leading to a term in the effective action
\frac{\mathcal{J}\bar{\mathcal{J}}}{\Sigma}
.
The momentum, angular momentum and string winding charges carried by the background arise from various cross-terms between the
U(1)_{y}
,
{SU(2)}
and
\mathds{R}_{t}
contributions to the null currents
\mathcal{J},\bar{\mathcal{J}}
; see
e.g.

Martinec:2017ztd
for further details.

6.2
The coset partition function

In this section we compute the partition function (
6.5
) of the coset CFT (
6.1
).

6.2.1
Fermionic sector

Let us begin by considering the contributions in the first line of (
6.5
).

\bm{SL(2,\mathds{R})}
:

The contribution of
\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})
fermions has already been computed in Section
4
and directly follows from eq. (
4.23
),

Z^{F}_{sl}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z)=e^{-\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}(u_{2}+\frac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}\frac{\big|\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\frac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z)\big|^{2}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{2}}\,{\rm exp}\Bigl(\tfrac{2}{\pi}\mathop{\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\int$}}}\nolimits\kern-0.99998pt\text{d}^{2}v\left|\partial_{v}\big(Y+\Phi[u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z]\big)\right|^{2}\Bigr)\ .

(6.8)

\bm{SU(2)}
:

The
SU(2)
fermionic action

S^{F}_{{su}}=\mathop{\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\int$}}}\nolimits\kern-0.99998pt\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\text d^2v}{2 \pi}$}}\left[\psi^{+}_{su}(\partial_{\bar{v}}+\ell_{2}A_{\bar{v}})\psi^{-}_{su}+\psi^{-}_{su}(\partial_{\bar{v}}-\ell_{2}A_{\bar{v}})\psi^{+}_{su}+\tilde{\psi}^{+}_{su}(\partial_{v}+r_{2}A_{v})\tilde{\psi}^{-}_{su}+\tilde{\psi}^{-}_{su}(\partial_{v}-r_{2}A_{v})\tilde{\psi}^{+}_{su}\right]

(6.9)

is gauge invariant under

\displaystyle\psi^{\pm}_{su}\mapsto e^{\pm\,\ell_{2}\,\gamma}\,\psi^{\pm}_{su}

\displaystyle,\qquad A_{v}\mapsto A_{v}+\partial_{v}\gamma\ ,

(6.10)

\displaystyle\tilde{\psi}^{\pm}_{su}\mapsto e^{\pm\,r_{2}\,\gamma}\,\tilde{\psi}^{\pm}_{su}

\displaystyle,\qquad A_{\bar{v}}\mapsto A_{\bar{v}}+\partial_{\bar{v}}\gamma\ .

As one would expect, for
\ell_{2}=r_{2}=1
eq. (
6.9
) reduces to eq. (
3.21
). In order to preserve gauge invariance at the quantum level, we assume the fermion path integral measure to be invariant under “rotations” of the form (
6.10
).
Performing a gauge transformation with parameter
\gamma=-X
, we can then eliminate
X
from the action (
6.10
) without picking up any anomaly factor. Following the same punchline of Section
3.2
, we introduce the fermions

\displaystyle\eta^{+}

\displaystyle=e^{\ell_{2}(-iY-i\Phi)}\psi^{+}_{su}\ ,

\displaystyle\eta^{-}

\displaystyle=e^{\ell_{2}(+iY+i\Phi)}\psi^{-}_{su}\ ,

(6.11)

\displaystyle\tilde{\eta}^{+}

\displaystyle=e^{r_{2}(iY+i\Phi)}\tilde{\psi}^{+}_{su}\ ,

\displaystyle\tilde{\eta}^{-}

\displaystyle=e^{r_{2}(-iY-i\Phi)}\tilde{\psi}^{-}_{su}\ .

As it was the case in Section
3.2
, this transformation produces an anomaly,

\mathcal{D}\psi^{+}_{su}\,\mathcal{D}\psi^{-}_{su}\,\mathcal{D}\tilde{\psi}^{+}_{su}\,\mathcal{D}\tilde{\psi}^{-}_{su}=\mathcal{D}\eta^{+}\,\mathcal{D}\eta^{-}\,\mathcal{D}\tilde{\eta}^{+}\,\mathcal{D}\tilde{\eta}^{-}\,e^{\check{\mathfrak{A}}}\ ,

(6.12)

which can formally be computed by promoting

\begin{pmatrix}\ 1\ &0\\
0&-1\ \end{pmatrix}\mapsto\begin{pmatrix}\ \ell_{2}\ &0\\
0&-r_{2}\ \end{pmatrix}

(6.13)

in (
3.26
) and in the various formulae for the axial anomaly. We find

\check{\mathfrak{A}}=\frac{i}{\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,(\ell_{2}\,\partial_{v}\alpha_{\ell}\,A_{\bar{v}}-r_{2}\,\partial_{\bar{v}}\alpha_{r}\,A_{v})=\frac{\ell^{2}_{2}+r^{2}_{2}}{\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])|^{2}\ ,

(6.14)

where

\alpha_{\ell}=\ell_{2}(-Y-\Phi)\ ,\qquad\alpha_{r}=-r_{2}(Y+\Phi)\ .

(6.15)

Let us recall that, as also discussed in Section
3
, one should shift the chemical potentials according to

\ell_{2}u\,\mapsto\,\ell_{2}u+\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z\ ,\qquad r_{2}\bar{u}\,\mapsto\,r_{2}\bar{u}+\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}\bar{z}\ .

(6.16)

As a result, the anomaly reads

\displaystyle\check{\mathfrak{A}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\ell_{2}^{2}+r_{2}^{2}}{\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])|^{2}+\frac{2}{\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}\Phi[\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z]|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\ell_{2}+r_{2}}{\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,\left[\partial_{v}\Phi[\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z]\,\partial_{\bar{v}}(Y+\Phi[u])+\partial_{\bar{v}}\Phi[\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z]\,\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])\right]

(6.17)

The path integral for the action (
6.9
) thus amounts to

Z^{F}_{su}=e^{\check{\mathfrak{A}}}\,Z_{\eta}\ ,

(6.18)

where we introduced the notation

Z_{\eta}=\int\mathcal{D}\eta^{+}\mathcal{D}\eta^{-}\mathcal{D}\tilde{\eta}^{+}\mathcal{D}\tilde{\eta}^{-}\,{\rm exp}\left(-\int\frac{\text{d}^{2}v}{2\pi}\left[\eta^{+}\partial_{\bar{v}}\eta^{-}+\eta^{-}\partial_{\bar{v}}\eta^{+}+\tilde{\eta}^{+}\partial_{\bar{v}}\tilde{\eta}^{-}+\tilde{\eta}^{-}\partial_{\bar{v}}\tilde{\eta}^{+}\right]\right)\ ,

(6.19)

and the fermions
\eta^{\pm},\tilde{\eta}^{\pm}
obey the boundary conditions

\displaystyle\eta^{\pm}(v+2\pi,\bar{v}+2\pi)

\displaystyle=e^{\pm 2\pi i\ell_{2}s_{1}}\,\eta^{\pm}(v,\bar{v})\ ,

(6.20)

\displaystyle\eta^{\pm}(v+2\pi\tau,\bar{v}+2\pi\bar{\tau})

\displaystyle=e^{\mp 2\pi i\ell_{2}s_{2}}\,\eta^{\pm}(v,\bar{v})\ ,

\displaystyle\tilde{\eta}^{\pm}(v+2\pi,\bar{v}+2\pi)

\displaystyle=e^{\mp 2\pi ir_{2}s_{1}}\,\tilde{\eta}^{\pm}(v,\bar{v})\ ,

\displaystyle\tilde{\eta}^{\pm}(v+2\pi\tau,\bar{v}+2\pi\bar{\tau})

\displaystyle=e^{\pm 2\pi ir_{2}s_{2}}\,\tilde{\eta}^{\pm}(v,\bar{v})\ .

Following
Alvarez-Gaume:1986rcs
, the partition function (
6.19
) reads

Z_{\eta}=e^{\pi i(\ell_{2}^{2}-r_{2}^{2})s_{1}s_{2}}\,e^{\frac{\pi iu_{2}^{2}}{\tau_{2}^{2}}(\ell^{2}_{2}\tau-r^{2}_{2}\bar{\tau})}\,\frac{\theta_{1}(\tau,\ell_{2}u)\,\overline{\theta_{1}(\tau,r_{2}u)}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{2}}\ .

(6.21)

Let us comment on the phase
e^{\pi i(\ell_{2}^{2}-r_{2}^{2})s_{1}s_{2}}
. This cannot be fixed by computing the partition function as a trace over the Hilbert space, and does not follow from the analysis of
Alvarez-Gaume:1986rcs
. We fix it by comparing with the free boson at the free fermion radius, see eq. (
C.39
).
16
16

16

Different Theta functions appear in eqs. (
3.30
) and (
C.39
), due to a different choice of spin structure.
Using that

e^{\pi i(\ell_{2}^{2}-r_{2}^{2})s_{1}s_{2}}\Biggl|_{\ell_{2}u\,\mapsto\,\ell_{2}u+\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z,\,r_{2}u\,\mapsto\,r_{2}u+\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z}=e^{\pi i(\ell_{2}^{2}-r_{2}^{2})s_{1}s_{2}}\,e^{\pi i\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}(\ell_{2}-r_{2})(s_{1}(z_{1}-\frac{z_{2}\tau_{1}}{\tau_{2}})+s_{2}\frac{z_{2}}{\tau_{2}})}\ ,

(6.22)

we finally obtain

\displaystyle Z^{F}_{su}

\displaystyle=\frac{e^{\pi i(\ell_{2}^{2}-r_{2}^{2})s_{1}s_{2}}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{2}}\,e^{\pi i\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}(\ell_{2}-r_{2})(s_{1}(z_{1}-\frac{z_{2}\tau_{1}}{\tau_{2}})+s_{2}\frac{z_{2}}{\tau_{2}})}\,e^{\frac{\pi i(\ell_{2}u_{2}+\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{\tau_{2}^{2}}\tau}\,e^{-\frac{\pi i(r_{2}u_{2}+\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{\tau_{2}^{2}}\bar{\tau}}

\displaystyle\times\,\theta_{1}(\tau,\ell_{2}\,u+\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z)\overline{\theta_{1}(\tau,r_{2}\,u+\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z)}

\displaystyle\times\,{\rm exp}\left(\frac{\ell_{2}^{2}+r_{2}^{2}}{\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}\left(Y+\Phi[u]\right)|^{2}+\frac{2}{\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v|\partial_{v}\Phi[\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z]|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\times\,{\rm exp}\left(\frac{\ell_{2}+r_{2}}{\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\left[\partial_{v}\Phi[\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z]\,\partial_{\bar{v}}(Y+\Phi[u])+\partial_{\bar{v}}\Phi[\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z]\,\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])\right]\right)\ .

(6.23)

Uncharged transverse fermions:

The two uncharged fermions in the “transverse” space that are not eliminated by the gauging have partition function

Z_{tr}^{F}=\frac{|\theta_{1}(\tau,z)|^{2}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{2}}\ .

(6.24)

6.2.2
Bosonic sector

Let us then consider the various contributions in the second line of (
6.5
).

\bm{SL(2,\mathds{R})}
:

The contribution from
SL(2,\mathds{R})_{n_{5}}
has already been computed in Section
4
and follows from eqs. (
4.13
) and (
4.16
). It reads

Z^{B}_{sl}(\tau,u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)=\frac{e^{\frac{2\pi(u_{2}+\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{\tau_{2}}}\,}{\sqrt{\tau_{2}}\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\frac{2}{n_{5}}z)|^{2}}\,{\rm exp}\Bigl(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{n_5+2}{\pi}$}}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,\bigl|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z])\bigr|^{2}\Bigr)\ .

(6.25)

\bm{U(1)}_{\bm{y}}
and
\mathds{R}_{\bm{t}}
:

Let us consider the contributions (
2.57
) for the
U(1)_{y}
boson. A short computation gives

S_{g_{y}}=\int\frac{\text{d}^{2}v}{\pi}\,\partial_{v}\left(y+r_{4}\Phi[u]\right)\partial_{\bar{v}}\left(y-\ell_{4}\Phi[u]\right)+\frac{(\ell_{4}+r_{4})^{2}}{4\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}\Phi[u]|^{2}

(6.26)

and

S^{X,Y}_{y}=-\frac{\ell_{4}^{2}+r_{4}^{2}}{2\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}(\Phi[u]+Y)|^{2}\ .

(6.27)

Using eqs. (
C.34
) and (
C.36
) we obtain

Z^{B}_{y}(\tau,u)=\frac{e^{\frac{\pi i}{2}(\ell_{4}^{2}-r_{4}^{2})s_{1}s_{2}}\,e^{\frac{\pi iu_{2}^{2}}{2\tau_{2}^{2}}(\ell_{4}^{2}\tau-r_{4}^{2}\bar{\tau})}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{2}}\,{\rm exp}\left(\frac{\ell^{2}_{4}+r^{2}_{4}}{2\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}Y+\partial_{v}\Phi[u]|^{2}\right)\\
\times\,\sum_{w,n\in\mathds{Z}}q^{\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{n}{R_{y}}+wR_{y}\right)^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{n}{R_{y}}-wR_{y}\right)^{2}}\,e^{\pi i\ell_{4}(\frac{n}{R_{y}}+wR_{y})u}\,e^{-\pi ir_{4}(\frac{n}{R_{y}}-wR_{y})\bar{u}}\ .

(6.28)

Similarly, we find

Z^{B}_{t}(\tau,u)=\frac{e^{\frac{\pi\,\ell^{2}_{3}\,u_{2}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{2}}\,{\rm exp}\left(-\frac{\ell_{3}^{2}}{\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])|^{2}\right)\,\int dE\,q^{-\frac{E^{2}}{4}}\,\bar{q}^{-\frac{E^{2}}{4}}\,e^{-2\pi u_{2}\ell_{3}E}\ .

(6.29)

\bm{SU(2)}
:

Here the computation becomes more involved. For
S_{g_{su}}
, using eq. (
D.22
) we obtain

\displaystyle\int\mathcal{D}g_{su}\,e^{-S_{g_{su}}}

\displaystyle=\int\mathcal{D}g_{su}\,{\rm exp}\left(-S^{\text{WZW}}(a^{-\varepsilon_{L}^{su}}\,g_{su}\,a^{\dagger\varepsilon_{R}^{su}})\right)\Bigr|_{\ell_{2}u\,\mapsto\,\ell_{2}u-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z,\,r_{2}u\,\mapsto\,r_{2}u-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z}

\displaystyle=\,e^{-\pi i\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}(\ell_{2}-r_{2})(s_{1}(z_{1}-\frac{z_{2}\tau_{1}}{\tau_{2}})+s_{2}\frac{z_{2}}{\tau_{2}})}\,e^{\frac{(n_{5}-2)\pi\,i\,(\ell_{2}\,u_{2}-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{2\tau_{2}^{2}}\tau}\,e^{-\frac{(n_{5}-2)\pi\,i\,(r_{2}\,u_{2}-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{2\tau_{2}^{2}}\bar{\tau}}\,

\displaystyle\quad\times\,e^{\pi i\frac{n_{5}-2}{2}(\ell_{2}^{2}-r_{2}^{2})s_{1}s_{2}}\,\mathcal{Z}_{B}^{\mathfrak{su}(2)}(\tau,\bar{\tau},\ell_{2}u-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z,r_{2}\bar{u}-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}\bar{z})\ ,

(6.30)

where we shifted the chemical potentials as described in Section
3
, see eq. (
3.6
). Let us now proceed with the computation of the two terms entering eq. (
2.57b
). The first one gives

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle S^{\text{WZW}}(a^{-\varepsilon_{L}^{su}}\,\Omega^{-\varepsilon_{L}^{su}}\,\Omega^{\dagger-\varepsilon_{R}^{su}}\,a^{\dagger-\varepsilon_{R}^{su}})\Bigr|_{\ell_{2}u\,\mapsto\,\ell_{2}u-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z,\,r_{2}u\,\mapsto\,r_{2}u-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z}

\displaystyle=-\frac{(n_{5}-2)(\ell_{2}-r_{2})^{2}}{4\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\left(|\partial_{v}X|^{2}+|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(n_{5}-2)(\ell_{2}-r_{2})}{4\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\left(\partial_{v}\Phi[-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z]\partial_{\bar{v}}(Y+\Phi[u])-\partial_{\bar{v}}\Phi[-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z]\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])\right)\ ,

(6.31)

while for the second we find

\displaystyle\frac{n_{5}-2}{2\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,\text{Tr}\Bigl[\varepsilon_{L}^{su}\,\partial_{v}a\,a^{-1}\,\varepsilon_{L}^{su}\partial_{\bar{v}}a\,a^{-1}+\varepsilon_{R}^{su}\,\partial_{v}a^{\dagger}a^{\dagger-1}\,\varepsilon_{R}^{su}\,\partial_{\bar{v}}a^{\dagger}a^{\dagger-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 120.0pt-2\,\varepsilon_{L}^{su}\,\partial_{\bar{v}}a\,a^{-1}\varepsilon_{R}^{su}\,\partial_{v}a^{\dagger}a^{\dagger-1}\Bigr]\Bigl|_{\ell_{2}u\,\mapsto\,\ell_{2}u-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z,\,r_{2}u\,\mapsto\,r_{2}u-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z}

\displaystyle=\frac{(n_{5}-2)(\ell_{2}-r_{2})^{2}}{4\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}X|^{2}-\frac{(n_{5}-2)(\ell_{2}+r_{2})^{2}}{4\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{(n_{5}-2)}{\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,|\partial_{v}\Phi[-\tfrac{2}{k}z]|^{2}-\frac{(n_{5}-2)(r_{2}+3\ell_{2})}{4\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,\partial_{v}\Phi[-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z]\partial_{\bar{v}}(Y+\Phi[u])

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{(n_{5}-2)(3r_{2}+\ell_{2})}{4\pi}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,\partial_{\bar{v}}\Phi[-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z]\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])\ .

(6.32)

Assembling eqs. (
6.30
), (
6.31
) and (
6.32
) we finally obtain

\displaystyle Z^{B}_{su}

\displaystyle(\tau,u-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z)=e^{\pi i\frac{n_{5}-2}{2}(\ell_{2}^{2}-r_{2}^{2})s_{1}s_{2}}\,e^{-\pi i\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}(\ell_{2}-r_{2})(s_{1}(z_{1}-\frac{z_{2}\tau_{1}}{\tau_{2}})+s_{2}\frac{z_{2}}{\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\times\,e^{\frac{(n_{5}-2)\pi\,i\,(\ell_{2}\,u_{2}-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{2\tau_{2}^{2}}\tau}\,e^{-\frac{(n_{5}-2)\pi\,i\,(r_{2}\,u_{2}-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{2\tau_{2}^{2}}\bar{\tau}}\,\mathcal{Z}_{B}^{\mathfrak{su}(2)}\left(\tau,\bar{\tau},\ell_{2}\,u-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z,r_{2}\,\bar{u}-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}\bar{z}\right)

\displaystyle\times\,{\rm exp}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(n_5-2)}{2\pi}$}}\int\text{d}^{2}v\,\left[(\ell_{2}^{2}+r_{2}^{2})|\partial_{v}Y+\partial_{v}\Phi[u]|^{2}+2|\partial_{v}\Phi[-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z]|^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\times\,{\rm exp}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(n_5-2)(\ell_2+r_2)}{2 \pi}$}}\int\text{d}^{2}v\left[\partial_{v}\Phi[-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z]\partial_{\bar{v}}(Y+\Phi[u])+\partial_{\bar{v}}\Phi[-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z]\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])\right]\right)\ .

(6.33)

Z^{ST}(\tau,z)=\frac{|\theta_{1}(\tau,z)|^{2}}{\sqrt{\tau_{2}}\,|\eta(\tau)|^{10}}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,\mathcal{I}_{u}\,\mathcal{I}_{Y}\,Z_{gh}(\tau,\bar{\tau})\,\mathcal{Z}_{B}^{\mathfrak{su}(2)}\left(\tau,\bar{\tau},\ell_{2}\,u-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z,r_{2}\,\bar{u}-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}\bar{z}\right)\\
\times\,\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}E\,q^{-\frac{E^{2}}{4}}\,\bar{q}^{-\frac{E^{2}}{4}}\,e^{-2\pi u_{2}\ell_{3}E}\,\sum_{w,n\in\mathds{Z}}q^{\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{n}{R_{y}}+wR_{y}\right)^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{n}{R_{y}}-wR_{y}\right)^{2}}\,e^{\pi i\ell_{4}(\frac{n}{R_{y}}+wR_{y})u}\,e^{-\pi ir_{4}(\frac{n}{R_{y}}-wR_{y})\bar{u}}\\

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{u}

\displaystyle=e^{2\pi\frac{(u_{2}+\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-\frac{2\pi}{\tau_{2}}(u_{2}+\frac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}\,e^{\frac{\pi\,\ell^{2}_{3}\,u_{2}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}}\,e^{\frac{\pi iu_{2}^{2}}{2\tau_{2}^{2}}(\ell_{4}^{2}\tau-r_{4}^{2}\bar{\tau})}

\displaystyle\qquad e^{\frac{(n_{5}-2)\pi\,i\,(\ell_{2}\,u_{2}-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{2\tau_{2}^{2}}\tau}\,e^{-\frac{(n_{5}-2)\pi\,i\,(r_{2}\,u_{2}-\frac{2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{2\tau_{2}^{2}}\bar{\tau}}\,e^{\frac{\pi i(\ell_{2}u_{2}+\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{\tau_{2}^{2}}\tau}\,e^{-\frac{\pi i(r_{2}u_{2}+\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z_{2})^{2}}{\tau_{2}^{2}}\bar{\tau}}

(6.35)

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{Y}={\rm exp}\Biggl(\int\text{d}^{2}v\Bigl[

\displaystyle-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{n_5+2}{\pi}$}}|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u+\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z])|^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{n_5-2}{2 \pi}$}}(\ell_{2}^{2}+r_{2}^{2})\,\big|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])\big|^{2}

\displaystyle-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\ell_3^2}{\pi}$}}|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])|^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\ell_4^2 + r_4^2}{2 \pi}$}}\,|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u])|^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{\pi}$}}\left|\partial_{v}(Y+\Phi[u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z])\right|^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\ell_2^2 + r_2^2}{\pi}$}}|\partial_{v}\left(Y+\Phi[u]\right)|^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{n_5-2}{\pi}$}}|\partial_{v}\Phi[-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z]|^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{\pi}$}}|\partial_{v}\Phi[\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z]|^{2}\Bigr]\Biggr)\ .

(6.36)

Using the null constraints (
6.3
), eq. (
6.35
) simplifies to

\mathcal{I}_{u}=e^{-\frac{4\pi}{\tau_{2}}z_{2}^{2}}\,e^{-\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-\pi n_{5}\tau_{2}s_{1}^{2}}\,e^{-4\pi z_{2}s_{1}}\ ,

while eq. (

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{Y}={\rm exp}\left(\frac{4\pi|z|^{2}}{\tau_{2}}\right)\ .

(6.38)

With these simplifications, the supertube partition function reads

Z^{ST}(\tau,z)=\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{2}}\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,z)|^{2}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{6}}\,e^{\frac{4\pi z_{1}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,\mathcal{I}(\tau,u,z)\ ,

(6.39a)

where we introduced the notation

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}(\tau,u,z)=

\displaystyle\,e^{-\pi n_{5}\tau_{2}s_{1}^{2}}\,e^{-4\pi z_{2}s_{1}}\,\mathcal{Z}_{B}^{\mathfrak{su}(2)}\left(\tau,\bar{\tau},\ell_{2}u-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z,r_{2}\bar{u}-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}\bar{z}\right)\,\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}E\,q^{-\frac{E^{2}}{4}}\,\bar{q}^{-\frac{E^{2}}{4}}\,e^{-2\pi u_{2}\ell_{3}E}

\displaystyle\times\,\frac{|\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\tfrac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}z)|^{2}\,\theta_{1}(\tau,\ell_{2}u+\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z)\,\overline{\theta_{1}(\tau,r_{2}u+\tfrac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}z)}}{|\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\frac{2}{n_{5}}z)|^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\,\sum_{w,n\in\mathds{Z}}q^{\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{n}{R_{y}}+wR_{y}\right)^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{n}{R_{y}}-wR_{y}\right)^{2}}\,e^{\pi i\ell_{4}(\frac{n}{R_{y}}+wR_{y})u}\,e^{-\pi ir_{4}(\frac{n}{R_{y}}-wR_{y})\bar{u}}\ .

(6.39b)

6.3
Evaluating the gauge field zero mode integrals

Let us now perform the integral over
s_{1}
and
s_{2}
in (
6.2.3
) following
Maldacena:2000kv
;
Hanany:2002ev
.
Formally, one would expect that the role of the Wilson lines for the gauge field is to enforce the zero mode parts of the gauge constraints. While we will see that this is eventually the correct picture, as reviewed in Section
4.3
there are a few subtleties arising from the fact that the action is quadratic rather than linear in the gauge fields.

Integral over
\bm{s_{2}}
:

Let us start by carrying out the integral over
s_{2}
in eq. (
6.2.3
). We remind the reader that
u
is related to
s_{1}
and
s_{2}
by eq. (
2.42
). We are going to see momentarily that the integral gives rise to a Kronecker delta, enforcing the difference of the left and right null conditions.

In order to make the dependence on
s_{2}
manifest, let us expand the various Theta functions entering (
6.2.3
). Using the identity (
4.34
) we can express the Theta functions in the denominator of (
6.39b
) as

\frac{1}{|\theta_{1}(\tau,u+\frac{2}{n_{5}}z)|^{2}}=\frac{1}{|\eta(\tau)|^{6}}\sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}S_{r}(\tau)\overline{S_{\bar{r}}(\tau)}\,e^{2\pi is_{1}\tau_{1}(r-\bar{r})}\,e^{-2\pi s_{1}\tau_{2}(r+\bar{r}+1)}\\
\times\,e^{\frac{4\pi i}{n_{5}}z_{1}(r-\bar{r})}\,e^{-\frac{4\pi}{n_{5}}z_{2}(r+\bar{r}+1)}\,e^{2\pi i(r-\bar{r})s_{2}}\ .

(6.40)

Using (
A.1
), (
B.2
) and (
B.10
), the
\mathfrak{su}(2)
partition function entering (
6.39b
) can be expanded as

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{B}^{\mathfrak{su}(2)}\left(\tau,\bar{\tau},\ell_{2}u-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}z,r_{2}\bar{u}-\tfrac{2}{n_{5}}\bar{z}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{|\eta(\tau)|^{2}}\,\sum_{j_{su}=0}^{n_{5}-2}\sum_{l,l^{\prime}=3-n_{5}}^{n_{5}-2}\chi^{\frac{\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{B,j_{su},l}(\tau)\,\overline{\chi^{\frac{\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{B,j_{su},l^{\prime}}(\tau)}\sum_{n_{b}^{su},\bar{n}_{b}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}}q^{(n_{5}-2)(n_{b}^{su}+\frac{l}{2(n_{5}-2)})^{2}}\,\bar{q}^{(n_{5}-2)(\bar{n}_{b}^{su}+\frac{l^{\prime}}{2(n_{5}-2)})^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\times\,e^{2\pi i(s_{1}\tau_{1}+s_{2})[\ell_{2}((n_{5}-2)n_{b}^{su}+\frac{l}{2})-r_{2}((n_{5}-2)\bar{n}_{b}^{su}+\frac{l^{\prime}}{2})]}\,e^{-4\pi i\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}[z(n_{b}^{su}+\frac{l}{2(n_{5}-2)})-\bar{z}(\bar{n}_{b}^{su}+\frac{l^{\prime}}{2(n_{5}-2)})]}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\times\,e^{-2\pi(n_{5}-2)s_{1}\tau_{2}(\ell_{2}n_{b}^{su}+r_{2}\bar{n}_{b}^{su}+\frac{\ell_{2}l+r_{2}l^{\prime}}{2(n_{5}-2)})}\ .

(6.41)

The other
\theta_{1}
functions entering eq. (
6.39b
) can be expressed in terms of their series definition (
A.2a
). Assembling all the contributions and making use of the quantization conditions (
6.6
), the supertube partition function (
6.2.3
) can be rewritten as

\displaystyle Z^{ST}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{2}}\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,z)|^{2}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{14}}\,e^{\frac{4\pi z_{1}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\,\tau_{2}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}E\sum_{w,n\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{su},\bar{n}_{f}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\,\sum_{j_{su}=0}^{n_{5}-2}\ \sum_{l,l^{\prime}=3-n_{5}}^{n_{5}-2}\ \sum_{n_{b}^{su},\bar{n}_{b}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}}\mathcal{I}_{sl}\,\mathcal{I}_{t,y}\,\mathcal{I}_{\theta}\,\mathcal{I}_{su}\,\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\,\mathcal{I}_{s_{1}}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,\mathcal{I}_{s_{2}}\ ,

(6.42)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{sl}

\displaystyle=S_{r}(\tau)\overline{S_{\bar{r}}(\tau)}\,e^{\frac{4\pi i}{n_{5}}z_{1}(r-\bar{r})}\,e^{-\frac{4\pi}{n_{5}}z_{2}(r+\bar{r}+1)}\ ,

(6.43a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{t,y}

\displaystyle=q^{-\frac{E^{2}}{4}}\,\bar{q}^{-\frac{E^{2}}{4}}\,q^{\frac{1}{4}(\frac{n}{R_{y}}+wR_{y})^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{4}(\frac{n}{R_{y}}-wR_{y})^{2}}\ ,

(6.43b)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\theta}

\displaystyle=(-1)^{n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}+n_{f}^{su}+\bar{n}_{f}^{su}}q^{\frac{1}{2}(n_{f}^{sl})^{2}+\frac{1}{2}(n_{f}^{su})^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{2}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})^{2}+\frac{1}{2}(\bar{n}_{f}^{sl})^{2}}\,e^{\frac{2\pi i(n_{5}+2)}{n_{5}}(n_{f}^{sl}z-\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\bar{z})+\frac{2\pi i(n_{5}-2)}{n_{5}}(n_{f}^{su}z-\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\bar{z})}\ ,

(6.43c)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{su}

\displaystyle=\chi^{\frac{\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{B,j_{su},l}(\tau)\,\overline{\chi^{\frac{\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{B,j_{su},l^{\prime}}(\tau)}\,\,q^{(n_{5}-2)(n_{b}^{su}+\frac{l}{2(n_{5}-2)})^{2}}\,\bar{q}^{(n_{5}-2)(\bar{n}_{b}^{su}+\frac{l^{\prime}}{2(n_{5}-2)})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt\times\,e^{-4\pi i\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}[z(n_{b}^{su}+\frac{l}{2(n_{5}-2)})-\bar{z}(\bar{n}_{b}^{su}+\frac{l^{\prime}}{2(n_{5}-2)})]}\ ,

(6.43d)

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{s_{1}}

\displaystyle=\,e^{-\pi n_{5}\tau_{2}s_{1}^{2}-4\pi z_{2}s_{1}}\,e^{2\pi is_{1}\tau_{1}(r-\bar{r}+\frac{\ell_{4}p_{L}-r_{4}p_{R}}{2}+n_{f}^{sl}-\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}+\ell_{2}\,n_{f}^{su}-r_{2}\,\bar{n}_{f}^{su}+\ell_{2}((n_{5}-2)n_{b}^{su}+\frac{l}{2})-r_{2}((n_{5}-2)\bar{n}_{b}^{su}+\frac{l^{\prime}}{2}))}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\,e^{-2\pi s_{1}\tau_{2}(r+\bar{r}+1+\ell_{3}E+\frac{\ell_{4}p_{L}+r_{4}p_{R}}{2}+n_{f}^{sl}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}+\ell_{2}\,n_{f}^{su}+r_{2}\,\bar{n}_{f}^{su}+(n_{5}-2)\ell_{2}n_{b}^{su}+(n_{5}-2)r_{2}\bar{n}_{b}^{su}+\frac{\ell_{2}l+r_{2}l^{\prime}}{2})}\ ,

(6.44)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{s_{2}}

\displaystyle=e^{2\pi is_{2}(r-\bar{r}+\frac{\ell_{4}p_{L}-r_{4}p_{R}}{2}+n_{f}^{sl}-\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}+\ell_{2}\,n_{f}^{su}-r_{2}\,\bar{n}_{f}^{su}+\ell_{2}((n_{5}-2)n_{b}^{su}+\frac{l}{2})-r_{2}((n_{5}-2)\bar{n}_{b}^{su}+\frac{l^{\prime}}{2}))}\ .

(6.45)

Notice that the supertube partition function (
6.42
) depends on
s_{2}
only through
\mathcal{I}_{s_{2}}
, see eq. (
6.45
). The quantization of
l_{i},r_{i}
, see eq. (
6.6
), and eq. (
B.11
) imply

\frac{1}{2}(\ell_{2}l-r_{2}l^{\prime})=\frac{1}{4}(\ell_{2}-r_{2})(l+l^{\prime})+\frac{1}{4}(\ell_{2}+r_{2})(l-l^{\prime})\in\mathds{Z}

(6.46)

and

\ell_{2}\,n_{f}^{su}-r_{2}\,\bar{n}_{f}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}\ ,\qquad\ell_{4}p_{L}-r_{4}p_{R}\in 2\mathds{Z}\ .

(6.47)

We can then use the Kronecker delta representation (
A.12
) and evaluate the integral over
s_{2}
,

\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{2}\,\mathcal{I}_{s_{2}}=\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\ ,

(6.48)

where making the identifications

(j^{3}_{sl})_{0}-(\bar{j}^{3}_{sl})_{0}=r-\bar{r}\ ,\qquad(j^{3}_{f,sl})_{0}=n_{f}^{sl}\ ,\qquad(\bar{j}^{3}_{f,sl})_{0}=\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\ ,

(6.49a)

(j^{3}_{su})_{0}=(n_{5}-2)n_{b}^{su}+\frac{l}{2}\ ,\quad(\bar{j}^{3}_{su})_{0}=(n_{5}-2)\bar{n}_{b}^{su}+\frac{l^{\prime}}{2}\ ,\quad(j^{3}_{f,su})_{0}=n_{f}^{su}\ ,\quad(\bar{j}^{3}_{f,su})_{0}=\bar{n}_{f}^{su}\ ,

(6.49b)

\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0}
is the difference of the null current zero modes

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{0}

\displaystyle=(J^{3}_{sl})^{~}_{0}+\ell_{2}(J^{3}_{su})^{~}_{0}+\ell_{3}\frac{E}{2}+\frac{\ell_{4}}{2}\,p^{~}_{L}\ ,

(6.50)

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0}

\displaystyle=(\bar{J}^{3}_{sl})^{~}_{0}+r_{2}(\bar{J}^{3}_{su})^{~}_{0}+\ell_{3}\frac{E}{2}+\frac{r_{4}}{2}\,p^{~}_{R}\ .

Thus, we see that the effect of the
s_{1}
integral is indeed to impose the zero mode of the axial null gauge constraints.

Integral over
\bm{s_{1}}
:

Following
Maldacena:2000kv
;
Hanany:2002ev
, a manipulation of the remaining
s_{1}
integral enables us to rewrite the partition function as a sum over discrete and continuous series representations of
SL(2,\mathds{R})
.

After carrying out the integral over
s_{2}
we are left with

\displaystyle Z^{ST}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{2}}\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,z)|^{2}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{14}}\,e^{\frac{4\pi z_{1}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\,\tau_{2}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}E\sum_{w,n\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{su},\bar{n}_{f}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\,\sum_{j_{su}=0}^{n_{5}-2}\ \sum_{l,l^{\prime}=3-n_{5}}^{n_{5}-2}\ \sum_{n_{b}^{su},\bar{n}_{b}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}}\mathcal{I}_{sl}\,\mathcal{I}_{t,y}\,\mathcal{I}_{\theta}\,\mathcal{I}_{su}\,\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\,\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\,\mathcal{I}_{s_{1}}\ ,

(6.51)

where
\mathcal{I}_{s_{1}}
collapsed to

\mathcal{I}_{s_{1}}=e^{-\pi n_{5}\tau_{2}s_{1}^{2}}\,e^{-4\pi z_{2}s_{1}}e^{-2\pi s_{1}\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}}\ ,

(6.52)

and we introduced the shorthand notation

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}

\displaystyle=r+\bar{r}+1+\mathcal{F}+\bar{\mathcal{F}}\ ,

(6.53a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{J}_{0}-(j^{3}_{sl})_{0}=\tfrac{1}{2}\ell_{3}E+\frac{\ell_{4}\,p_{L}}{2}+n_{f}^{sl}+\ell_{2}\,n_{f}^{su}+(n_{5}-2)\ell_{2}n_{b}^{su}+\tfrac{1}{2}\ell_{2}l\ ,

(6.53b)

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{F}}

\displaystyle=\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0}-(\bar{j}^{3}_{sl})_{0}=\tfrac{1}{2}\ell_{3}E+\frac{r_{4}\,p_{R}}{2}+\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}+r_{2}\,\bar{n}_{f}^{su}+(n_{5}-2)r_{2}\bar{n}_{b}^{su}+\tfrac{1}{2}r_{2}l^{\prime}\ .

(6.53c)

Let us now proceed as in Section
4.3
and complete the square in eq. (
6.52
),

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\,\mathcal{I}_{s_{1}}

\displaystyle=e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\,{\rm exp}\left(-\pi n_{5}\tau_{2}(s_{1}+\tfrac{2z_{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}})^{2}-2\pi s_{1}\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}\right)

\displaystyle=e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}+\frac{4\pi z_{2}}{n_{5}}\mathcal{K}}\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\,{\rm exp}\left(-\pi n_{5}\tau_{2}s_{1}^{2}-2\pi s_{1}\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}\right)\ ,

(6.54)

where in the second equality we exploited the periodicity properties discussed in Appendix
F
, see eq. (
F.10
). Using the identity (
4.43
) it is easy to evaluate the integral over
s_{1}
. We are left with

\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\,\mathcal{I}_{s_{1}}=-\frac{e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}+\frac{4\pi z_{2}}{n_{5}}\mathcal{K}}}{2\pi\sqrt{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\Biggl(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi\,c^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-2\pi(ic+\tau_{2}\mathcal{K})}}{ic+\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}}-\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi\,c^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}}{ic+\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}}\Biggr)\ .

(6.55)

Consider the first integral in the right-hand-side of eq. (
6.55
) and similarly to what we did in Section
4.3
let us shift the integration contour vertically from
\text{Im}\,c=0
to
\text{Im}\,c=-in_{5}\tau_{2}
, see Figure
1
. We obtain

\int_{0}^{1}\text{d}s_{1}\,\mathcal{I}_{s_{1}}=\mathcal{I}_{dis}+\mathcal{I}_{con}\ ,

(6.56)

with

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{dis}

\displaystyle=-\frac{e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}}{n_{5}}\mathcal{K}}}{2\pi\sqrt{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,\oint_{\Gamma_{dis}}\text{d}c\,\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi\,c^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-2\pi(ic+\tau_{2}\mathcal{K})}}{ic+\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}}\ ,

(6.57)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{con}

\displaystyle=-\frac{e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}}{n_{5}}\mathcal{K}}}{2\pi\sqrt{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,\Biggl(\int_{-\infty-i\tau_{2}n_{5}}^{\infty-i\tau_{2}n_{5}}\text{d}c\,\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi\,c^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-2\pi(ic+\tau_{2}\mathcal{K})}}{ic+\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}}-\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi\,c^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}}{ic+\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}}\Biggr)\ ,

(6.58)

where the integral in (
6.57
) is evaluated along the clockwise contour
\Gamma_{dis}
encircling the region

-\tau_{2}n_{5}<\text{Im }c<0\ ,

(6.59)

see Figure
1
with
\kappa_{sl}=n_{5}
. Similarly to Section
4.3
, the split of integration contours induces for the partition function the split

Z^{ST}(\tau,z)=Z^{ST}_{dis}(\tau,z)+Z^{ST}_{con}(\tau,z)\ .

(6.60)

We are going to see that, as the notation should suggest,
Z^{ST}_{dis}
will reproduce the short string spectrum while
Z^{ST}_{con}(\tau,z)
captures the long string spectrum. Let us analyze these two contributions one after the other.

Short string spectrum:

We start from the short string contribution to the partition function and evaluate the contour integral (
6.57
) by means of Cauchy’s theorem. Rewriting
c
as in eq. (
4.51
), eq. (
6.57
) reads

\mathcal{I}_{dis}=i\frac{e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}+\frac{4\pi z_{2}}{n_{5}}\mathcal{K}}}{\pi\sqrt{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,\oint_{\Gamma_{dis}}\text{d}j_{sl}\,\,\frac{e^{\frac{\pi\,\tau_{2}\,(2j_{sl}-1)^{2}}{n_{5}}}\,e^{-2\pi\tau_{2}(2j_{sl}-1+\mathcal{K})}}{2j_{sl}-1+\mathcal{K}}\ .

(6.61)

Exactly as in Section
4.3
, poles only appear for real
j_{sl}
and in the region corresponding to the Maldacena-Ooguri bound. Also in this case, the denominator of the integrand in (
6.61
) equals the sum of the null current zero modes (
6.50
),

2j_{sl}-1+\mathcal{K}=\mathcal{J}_{0}+\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0}\ ,

(6.62)

where we used eq. (
6.53
) and made the identifications

(j^{3}_{sl})_{0}=r+j_{sl}\ ,\qquad(\bar{j}^{3}_{sl})_{0}=\bar{r}+j_{sl}\ .

(6.63)

These identifications are consistent with eq. (
6.49b
) and provide the correct quantization of quantum numbers for the discrete representation of
\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})
. We can then rewrite the integral
\mathcal{I}_{dis}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{dis}=-\frac{2\,e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}}{\sqrt{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,(q\bar{q})^{-\frac{1}{4n_{5}}}\,\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\frac{n_{5}+1}{2}}\text{d}j_{sl}\,\delta(\mathcal{J}_{0}+\mathcal{J}_{0})\,q^{-\frac{j_{sl}(j_{sl}-1)}{n_{5}}}\,\bar{q}^{-\frac{j_{sl}(j_{sl}-1)}{n_{5}}}\,e^{-\frac{4\pi(2j_{sl}-1)\,z_{2}}{n_{5}}}\ .

(6.64)

Thus, the short string contribution to the partition function reads

\displaystyle Z^{ST}_{dis}(\tau,z)=\frac{e^{\frac{4\pi z_{1}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}}\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,z)|^{2}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{14}}\,\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}E\sum_{w,n\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{su},\bar{n}_{f}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\ \sum_{j_{su}=0}^{n_{5}-2}\ \sum_{l,l^{\prime}=3-n_{5}}^{n_{5}-2}\sum_{n_{b}^{su},\bar{n}_{b}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\,(q\bar{q})^{-\frac{1}{4n_{5}}}\,\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\frac{n_{5}+1}{2}}\text{d}j_{sl}\sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}(q\bar{q})^{-\frac{j_{sl}(j_{sl}-1)}{n_{5}}}\,S_{r}(\tau)\,\overline{S_{\bar{r}}(\tau)}\,e^{2\pi iz\,\frac{2}{n_{5}}(j^{3}_{sl})_{0}}e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}\,\frac{2}{n_{5}}(\bar{j}^{3}_{sl})_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\,\mathcal{I}_{\theta}\,\mathcal{I}_{t,y}\,\mathcal{I}_{su}\,\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\,\delta(\mathcal{J}_{0}+\mathcal{J}_{0})\ ,

(6.65)

where we reabsorbed some
n_{5}
factors into the overall normalization and observed that in terms of the identifications (
6.63
) we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{t,y}

\displaystyle=q^{-\frac{E^{2}}{4}}\,\bar{q}^{-\frac{E^{2}}{4}}\,q^{\frac{1}{4}(\frac{n}{R_{y}}+wR_{y})^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{4}(\frac{n}{R_{y}}-wR_{y})^{2}}\ ,

(6.66)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\theta}

\displaystyle=(-1)^{F}q^{\frac{1}{2}((j^{3}_{f,sl})_{0})^{2}+\frac{1}{2}((j^{3}_{f,su})_{0})^{2}}\bar{q}^{\frac{1}{2}((\bar{j}^{3}_{f,sl})_{0})^{2}+\frac{1}{2}((\bar{j}^{3}_{f,sl})_{0})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\,e^{2\pi iz\frac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}(j^{3}_{f,sl})_{0}}\,e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}\frac{n_{5}+2}{n_{5}}(\bar{j}^{3}_{f,sl})_{0}}e^{2\pi iz\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}(j^{3}_{f,su})_{0}}e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}\frac{n_{5}-2}{n_{5}}(\bar{j}^{3}_{f,sl})_{0}}\ ,

(6.67)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{su}

\displaystyle=\chi^{\frac{\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{B,j_{su},l}(\tau)\,\overline{\chi^{\frac{\mathfrak{su}(2)}{\mathfrak{u}(1)}}_{B,j_{su},l^{\prime}}(\tau)}\,\,q^{\frac{(j^{3}_{su})_{0}^{2}}{(n_{5}-2)}}\,\bar{q}^{\frac{(\bar{j}^{3}_{su})_{0}^{2}}{(n_{5}-2)}}\,e^{-2\pi iz\frac{2}{n_{5}}(j^{3}_{su})_{0}}\,e^{2\pi i\bar{z}\frac{2}{n_{5}}(j^{3}_{su})_{0}}\ .

(6.68)

Using eqs. (
3.3
) and (
4.5
), it is now manifest that the discrete part of the partition function can be written as a trace over a constrained Hilbert space

Z^{ST}_{dis}(\tau,z)=e^{\frac{4\pi z_{1}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}}\,\text{Tr}_{\mathcal{H}\otimes\bar{\mathcal{H}}}\,q^{L_{0}-\frac{c}{24}}\,\bar{q}^{\bar{L}_{0}-\frac{c}{24}}\,e^{2\pi izJ^{\mathcal{R}}_{0}}\,e^{-2\pi i\bar{z}\bar{J}^{\mathcal{R}}_{0}}\Bigl|_{\mathcal{J}_{0}=\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0}=0}\ .

(6.69)

The holomorphic Hilbert space
\mathcal{H}
(and similarly for
\bar{\mathcal{H}}
) is defined by tensoring
\mathcal{D}^{+}_{j_{sl}}
representations of
\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathds{R})_{n_{5}}
with representations of the other factors entering the numerator
G
of the coset theory (
6.1
). The exponential prefactor in eq. (
6.69
) is the expected factor relating path integral and trace partition functions, see
Kraus:2006nb
and Appendix
D
. Eq. (
6.69
) confirms that the partition function (
6.2.3
) reproduces the expected short string spectrum, confirming the analysis carried out in
Martinec:2018nco
;
Bufalini:2021ndn
in the operator formalism.

Long string spectrum:

Let us now consider the second term in (
6.60
) and show how the long string spectrum emerges after manipulating the integral
\mathcal{I}_{con}
defined in eq. (
6.58
). Performing a simple change of variable in the first integral, we can write eq. (
6.58
) as the sum of the two contributions,

\mathcal{I}_{con}=\mathcal{I}_{con}^{-}+\mathcal{I}_{con}^{+}\ ,

(6.70)

defined as

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{con}^{-}

\displaystyle=-\frac{e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}}{n_{5}}\mathcal{K}}}{2\pi\sqrt{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi\,c^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-\pi\tau_{2}n_{5}-2\pi\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}}}{ic+n_{5}\tau_{2}+\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}}\ ,

(6.71)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{con}^{+}

\displaystyle=\frac{e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}}{n_{5}}\mathcal{K}}}{2\pi\sqrt{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi\,c^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}}{ic+\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}}\ .

(6.72)

Following the strategy reviewed in Section
4.3
, we want to manipulate
\mathcal{I}_{con}^{-}
so that the numerator of the integrand becomes similar to the one in
\mathcal{I}_{con}^{+}
. In order to do so, let us split the long string contribution to the partition function (
6.60
) as

Z^{ST}_{con}(\tau,z)=Z^{ST,+}_{con}(\tau,z)+Z^{ST,-}_{con}(\tau,z)\ ,

(6.73)

where we defined

\displaystyle Z^{ST,\pm}_{con}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{2}}\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,z)|^{2}}{|\eta(\tau)|^{14}}\,e^{\frac{4\pi z_{1}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}}\,e^{-\frac{4\pi z_{2}^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}E\sum_{w,n\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{su},\bar{n}_{f}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\,\sum_{j_{su}=0}^{n_{5}-2}\ \sum_{l,l^{\prime}=3-n_{5}}^{n_{5}-2}\mathcal{I}_{sl}\ \sum_{n_{b}^{su},\bar{n}_{b}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}}\,\mathcal{I}_{t,y}\,\mathcal{I}_{\theta}\,\mathcal{I}_{su}\,\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\,\,\mathcal{I}_{con}^{\pm}\,,

(6.74)

and
\mathcal{I}_{sl}
,
\mathcal{I}_{t,y}
,
\mathcal{I}_{\theta}
and
\mathcal{I}_{su}
are still given by eqs. (
6.43
). In order to cancel the factor
e^{-\pi\tau_{2}n_{5}-2\pi\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}}
in the numerator of
\mathcal{I}^{-}_{con}
, let us shift the various summation labels entering eq. (
6.74
) according to

w\to w+\text{k}\ ,\quad n\to n-\text{p}\ ,\quad n_{f}^{sl}\to n_{f}^{sl}-1\ ,\quad\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\to\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}-1\ ,\quad E\to E+\ell_{3}\ ,

(6.75a)

n_{f}^{su}\to n_{f}^{su}-\ell_{2}\ ,\quad\bar{n}_{f}^{su}\to\bar{n}_{f}^{su}-r_{2}\ ,\quad n_{b}^{su}\to n_{b}^{su}-\frac{\ell_{2}}{2}\ ,\quad\bar{n}_{b}^{su}\to\bar{n}_{b}^{su}-\frac{r_{2}}{2}\ .

(6.75b)

Notice that due to the null conditions (
6.3
) the Kronecker delta
\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}
is unaffected by the shifts above. Using once more the null conditions (
6.3
) and exploiting the presence of the Kronecker delta, it is tedious but straightforward to check that the net effect of these shifts is producing the extra term

q^{r}\,\bar{q}^{\bar{r}}\,e^{\pi\tau_{2}n_{5}+2\pi\tau_{2}\mathcal{K}}\ ,

(6.76)

and adding
-(n_{5}+2)\tau_{2}
to the denominator of the integrand. Eq. (
6.74
) for
Z^{ST,-}_{con}(\tau,z)
thus becomes

\displaystyle Z^{ST,-}_{con}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=-\frac{e^{\frac{4\pi z_{1}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}}\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,z)|^{2}}{2\pi\sqrt{n_{5}}\,|\eta(\tau)|^{14}}\,\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}E\sum_{w,n\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{su},\bar{n}_{f}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\,\sum_{j_{su}=0}^{n_{5}-2}\ \sum_{l,l^{\prime}=3-n_{5}}^{n_{5}-2}\ \sum_{n_{b}^{su},\bar{n}_{b}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}S_{r}(\tau)\,\overline{S_{\bar{r}}(\tau)}\,e^{-\frac{4\pi iz_{1}}{n_{5}}(r-\bar{r})}\,\mathcal{I}_{t,y}\,\mathcal{I}_{\theta}\,\mathcal{I}_{su}

\displaystyle\quad\times\,\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\,\,e^{\frac{4\pi z_{2}}{n_{5}}(\mathcal{F}+\bar{\mathcal{F}})}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,\frac{e^{-\frac{\pi\,c^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}}{ic-\tau_{2}(1+r+\bar{r})+\tau_{2}(\mathcal{F}+\bar{\mathcal{F}})}\,,

(6.77)

where we used eqs. (
6.53
) to rewrite
\mathcal{K}
in terms of
\mathcal{F}
and
\bar{\mathcal{F}}
and the identity (
4.66
) to change the sign of the summation labels
r
and
\bar{r}
. Notice that using eqs. (
6.49b
) and (
6.53
) the difference of null currents can be written as

\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0}=\mathcal{F}-\bar{\mathcal{F}}-(r-\bar{r})

(6.78)

and hence

\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\,e^{-\frac{4\pi iz_{1}}{n_{5}}(r-\bar{r})}=\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}\,e^{-\frac{4\pi iz_{1}}{n_{5}}(\mathcal{F}-\bar{\mathcal{F}})}\ .

(6.79)

The long string contribution to the partition function (
6.73
) then reads

\displaystyle Z^{ST}_{con}(\tau,z)

\displaystyle=\frac{e^{\frac{4\pi z_{1}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}}\,|\theta_{1}(\tau,z)|^{2}}{2\pi\sqrt{n_{5}}\,|\eta(\tau)|^{14}}\,\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}E\sum_{w,n\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{sl},\bar{n}_{f}^{sl}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}\ \sum_{n_{f}^{su},\bar{n}_{f}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}+\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\,\sum_{j_{su}=0}^{n_{5}-2}\ \sum_{l,l^{\prime}=3-n_{5}}^{n_{5}-2}\ \sum_{n_{b}^{su},\bar{n}_{b}^{su}\in\mathds{Z}}\ \sum_{r,\bar{r}\in\mathds{Z}}S_{r}(\tau)\,\overline{S_{\bar{r}}(\tau)}\,\mathcal{I}_{t,y}\,\mathcal{I}_{\theta}\,\mathcal{I}_{su}\,\xi^{-\frac{2}{n_{5}}\mathcal{F}}\,\bar{\xi}^{-\frac{2}{n_{5}}\bar{\mathcal{F}}}\,\delta_{\mathcal{J}_{0}-\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{0},0}

\displaystyle\times\,\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\text{d}c\,\,e^{-\frac{\pi\,c^{2}}{n_{5}\tau_{2}}}\,\biggl(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{i c +\tau_2(r + \bar r +1)+ \tau_2 \mathcal F + \tau_2 \bar{\mathcal F}}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{i c - \tau_2(r + \bar r +1) + \tau_2 \mathcal F + \tau_2 \bar{\mathcal F}}$}}\biggr)\ ,

(6.80)
